# Wizards Only Fools Roadmap Progress

This tracker keeps the current seven-part cleanup/engine roadmap in one place. The open-world scale is intentional: item 6 is about smoother architecture and runtime cost, not making the world smaller.

## Status Key

- Not started: no meaningful code work yet.
- Started: first code or verification pass exists.
- In progress: feature has working pieces, but still needs follow-up passes.
- Complete for now: current requested scope is done, with future polish possible.

## 1. Split Game Systems Into Clear Boundaries

Status: In progress

Done:
- Added `src/game/systems/systemCatalog.ts` as the top-level boundary map.
- Added a spell catalog boundary under `src/game/systems/spells/`.
- Moved the `qaHideManaFlowers` route flag behind `manaRechargeRuntime.ts`, so the rune renderer consumes a spell-owned helper instead of parsing browser search directly.
- Added a placeable catalog and shared placeable model boundary under `src/game/systems/placeables/`.
- Extracted engine-placed object rendering and placement validation from `GameWorld.tsx` into `src/game/systems/placeables/EnginePlacedObjects.tsx`.
- Extracted engine placement target planning, grid snapping, slope validation, yaw selection, and training-dummy spawn planning into `src/game/systems/placeables/placementRules.ts`.
- Extracted engine placeable footprint overlap checks into `src/game/systems/placeables/enginePlacementCollision.ts`.
- Extracted shared placeable building metrics and footprint bounds into `src/game/systems/placeables/placeableMetrics.ts`.
- Moved touch-device detection out of `HUD.tsx` and into the shared performance/input helper layer.
- Moved dev fast-travel spawn metadata out of `HUD.tsx` and into `src/game/tools/devFastTravel.ts`.

- Extracted base village perimeter walls and closed arena walls into `src/game/systems/world/villages/`.
- Extracted base village terrain height, terrain mesh, and terrain collider ownership into `src/game/systems/world/terrain/`.

- Extracted the character customization live preview canvas into a lazy `CharacterPreview.tsx` module so normal gameplay HUD does not load avatar preview drawing code.

- Moved status spell speed/config/type guards into `src/game/systems/spells/statusSpellConfig.ts`.
- Moved projectile sprite URL lists, anonymous texture preloading, cache ownership, and fallback texture handling into `src/game/systems/spells/spellProjectileAssets.ts`.
- Reworked projectile sprite asset lists into verified explicit path manifests and direct preload/cache loops, preserving anonymous texture loading plus fallback error handling without setup-time `Array.from`/`forEach` work.

- Moved projectile seeded-random, aim-direction, hand-selection, and cylinder-segment math helpers into `src/game/systems/spells/spellProjectileMath.ts`.

- Moved local/remote projectile ownership checks and QA projectile creator ID reads into `src/game/systems/spells/spellProjectileOwnership.ts`.
- Moved the route-gated spell dummy QA scene component into `src/game/systems/spells/spellDummyQaScene.tsx`, leaving `Projectiles.tsx` to mount the QA scene without owning dummy state, F9 sequencing, or dummy HUD labels.
- Moved player spell spawn positioning, hand offsets, direct-status aim rays, and remote-player aim-cone targeting into `src/game/systems/spells/playerSpellCasting.ts`, leaving `PlayerController.tsx` to request launches instead of owning combat targeting math.
- Routed the remaining QA practice-cast and flamethrower spawn origins through `playerSpellCasting.ts`, removing the last raw spell-spawn offset math from `PlayerController.tsx` while preserving flamethrower's pre-spread lateral hand offset.
- Extracted mana recharge runtime helpers into `src/game/systems/spells/manaRechargeRuntime.ts`, including rune cycling, mana-flower cooldown pruning, direct-loop QA dataset publishing, and shared respawn timing.
- Added explicit survival mana-source resolver readiness checks so `Runes.tsx` no longer caches an empty source list before terrain/village resolvers are configured for the current survival chunk.
- Moved portal projectile spawning, active portal rendering/teleport collision, and blink visuals into `src/game/systems/spells/spellPortalTraversal.tsx`, leaving `Projectiles.tsx` to mount traversal spells through a narrow component boundary.
- Moved disc/orb shield rendering, shield material setup, shield collision groups, and shield tracking physics into `src/game/systems/spells/spellShieldRendering.tsx`.
- Moved grab spell targeting, release handling, force dispatch, and arm/finger segment rendering into `src/game/systems/spells/spellGrabRendering.tsx`.
- Reworked grab spell arm/finger index setup and arm path-point scratch setup to use direct-loop builders instead of deterministic `Array.from` allocations while keeping the same arm, palm, and finger rendering behavior.
- Moved tornado and meteor area-effect lifecycles, seeded visual particles, mobile visual throttling, area dummy hits, screen shake, and local-player impact logic into `src/game/systems/spells/spellAreaEffects.tsx`.
- Moved smoke bomb collision/cloud rendering into `src/game/systems/spells/spellSmokeRendering.tsx`.

- Moved touch fullscreen state/request handling into `src/game/systems/input/fullscreenRuntime.ts` so controller face buttons no longer share the mobile fullscreen path.
- Added a rendering-owned ResizeObserver fallback and explicit Canvas resize polyfill so local QA routes reliably hand R3F the app-frame size instead of occasionally stalling at the default 300x150 canvas.
- Tightened canvas runtime QA telemetry so the resize observer reports observed/delivered frame rects and the frame probe can publish the first rendered frame instead of waiting for the tenth frame.
- Added rendering-owned real WebGL canvas element sizing telemetry so QA routes can verify the actual Three.js `<canvas>` fills the app frame, not just the R3F wrapper.
- Changed the forced ResizeObserver fallback to deliver only changed target rects/DPR values, keeping immediate QA sizing recovery without calling canvas resize handlers every 250 ms when the app frame is stable.
- Moved pure HUD directional menu focus solving into `hudMenuNavigation.ts` and settings-panel index/action-count constants into `hudSettingsPanelConfig.ts`, leaving `HUD.tsx` to orchestrate runtime state instead of owning derived menu math.
- Moved magic-hand sprite sizing, palm anchor constants, CORS-safe hand image loading, transparency cleanup, pixel filtering, and fallback hand drawing into `MagicHandSpriteCanvas.tsx`.
- Moved magic-hand frame cycling, rune readiness, firing-pose timing, charge-state reads, and aspect-offset class selection into `useMagicHandsPose.ts`.
- Changed magic-hand equip-scale and Fireball equip-frame transitions from one-step intervals to one-step timeouts, keeping the same cadence without persistent interval handles between React ticks.
- Moved magic-hand held-spell visual canvases, GIF overlays, buff glyphs, kunai/orb Three.js previews, and spell-effect selection into `MagicHandSpellEffects.tsx`, leaving `MagicHands.tsx` to compose hand layers instead of owning every spell renderer.
- Extracted magic-hand spell-effect pixel cleanup, magic-glass-orb target signal solving, and tiny tornado pixel-block data into `magicHandSpellEffectsRuntime.ts`.
- Reused the hidden Kunai hand-effect scratch canvas across RAF ticks so the 3D-to-pixel HUD pass no longer allocates a new downsample canvas every frame.
- Reused fixed Lightning/Chidori bolt point buffers across RAF updates so the hand-effect canvas no longer rebuilds nested point arrays during live gameplay.
- Split Blink, Smoke Bomb, and Portal held-hand GIF overlays into `magicHandGifSpellEffects.tsx`, leaving `MagicHandSpellEffects.tsx` to route effect families instead of owning every overlay implementation inline.
- Moved buff/status hand-effect glow, block, spark, and animation-duration data into `magicHandBuffSpellEffectsRuntime.ts`, keeping buff visuals tunable without editing the renderer body.
- Moved buff/status hand-effect glyph rendering into `magicHandBuffSpellEffects.tsx`, leaving the main magic-hand effect file to route the buff family through one component.
- Split Tornado and Meteor Shower held-hand area effects into `magicHandAreaSpellEffects.tsx` and moved shared hand-effect rectangle rendering into `magicHandPixelBlocks.tsx`.
- Extracted mobile touch-control joystick math, hotbar wheel direction helpers, RAF-batched move/look dispatch, and button/cast event wiring into `mobileTouchControlsRuntime.ts`, leaving `MobileTouchControls.tsx` to own the visible control layout.
- Moved the HUD engine placeable preview/request/clear/edit/delete dispatch bridge into `hudEnginePlaceableRuntime.ts`, leaving `HUD.tsx` with thin menu callbacks while the placeable event payload shape stays isolated.
- Moved HUD lazy panel registration into `hudLazyModules.ts`, so optional HUD surfaces are declared in one place instead of inside the main HUD runtime.
- Moved the video/keybind/voice/character settings UI into lazy `SettingsPanel.tsx`, leaving `HUD.tsx` to orchestrate state while the settings surface owns its own rendering and readability work.
- Moved responsive settings panel and scroll-panel style derivation into static helpers in `settingsPanelRuntime.ts` / `SettingsPanel.tsx`, so the parent HUD no longer allocates settings-only style objects during ordinary gameplay renders.
- Moved character customization update/cycling rules into `characterCustomizationRuntime.ts`, so `HUD.tsx` no longer owns color preset stepping, style option stepping, or character row lookups while `SettingsPanel.tsx` keeps the same live controls.
- Moved HUD hold timing/default look sensitivity into `hudInputConfig.ts` and reused the shared input-system number-slot parser for HUD and quest-editor shortcuts.
- Moved pixel-avatar canvas drawing, character normalization, animation timing, billboard direction, and texture caching into `avatarTextureFactory.ts`, leaving `PixelAvatar.tsx` to own React/Three component wiring.
- Moved optional world module registration for the live minimap and network manager into `gameWorldLazyModules.ts`, keeping `GameWorld.tsx` focused on world composition rather than lazy import ownership.
- Added cached hex-to-RGB terrain color parsing plus scratch-capable survival terrain color sampling, then routed smoothed terrain/grass color sampling through the scratch helper to reduce repeated allocations during terrain and vegetation builds.
- Lazy-loaded authored survival village renderers from the procedural world renderer and moved desert/swamp/graveyard/mountain surface helpers into lightweight village terrain modules, reducing the main `GameWorld` chunk to about 2,821.29 kB minified / 1,016.21 kB gzip while keeping villages as separate chunks that mount when their chunks stream in.
- Added a background authored-village renderer preloader keyed by streamed chunk metadata, so split village chunks can start loading before React reaches their lazy mount and publishes `wofSurvivalVillageRendererPreloads` for QA checks.
- Lazy-loaded optional scatter scenery for desert landmarks, roof-forest hobbit huts, and dormant legacy wildflowers behind local suspense boundaries, trimming the main `GameWorld` chunk to about 2,805.16 kB minified / 1,012.46 kB gzip while keeping common trees, underbrush, insects, birds, and rocks eager for nearby terrain readability.
- Lazy-loaded the spell-dummy QA scene through the world lazy-module registry, so ordinary survival play no longer eagerly loads `qaSpellDummies=1` test rendering and the scene splits into its own about 6.74 kB minified / 2.62 kB gzip chunk.
- Added scratch-capable smoothed/rendered terrain color and grass blade color helpers, wired BOTW grass generation to reuse intermediate color objects, and removed per-vertex color cloning during cached base-village terrain mesh generation.
- Added scratch-capable smoothed terrain-color access through the BOTW grass resolver contract so grass carpet builds reuse one terrain-color sample object instead of allocating per accepted quad.
- Reworked survival river, BOTW carpet, cached grass/flower primitives, graveyard ramp collider, and mountain trail/deck/summit geometry generation to use fixed typed buffers instead of push-grown arrays where counts are deterministic or bounded.
- Reworked cached solid-tree visual assembly to reuse scratch vectors/colors for trunk, branch, and canopy lobe composition instead of allocating temporary branch/lobe objects during tree geometry warmup.
- Extracted base-village props, terrain, walls, water plane, villagers, ripples, and treehouse into lazy `BaseVillageScene.tsx`, and lazy-loaded persistent quest NPCs from the world lazy-module registry, dropping the main `GameWorld` chunk below 1 MB gzip while keeping base-village detail phasing intact.
- Lazy-loaded the `Projectiles` router from the world lazy-module registry so spell visual/rendering code no longer lives in the main world shell, creating a separate about 46.89 kB minified / 13.70 kB gzip projectile chunk.
- Lazy-loaded and dev-gated the engine placement runtime behind the same developer-tool availability used by the HUD, splitting `EnginePlacedObjects`, `PlaceableModel`, and the placeable catalog away from ordinary survival play.
- Moved base-village wall and ground texture setup into `BaseVillageScene.tsx`, so `GameWorld.tsx` only decides whether the base village exists and no longer clones village terrain detail textures while the player is out in the open world.
- Lazy-loaded the classic non-survival Drei sky/environment into `ClassicSkyEnvironment.tsx`, keeping survival-focused world startup from carrying the sunset environment preset path.
- Lazy-loaded `Runes` from the world module registry and split base-village hut layout data into `baseVillageHutLayout.ts`, so rune and villager data reads no longer import the heavy hut renderer/CSG/collider module.
- Extracted deterministic base-village hut texture factories into `baseVillageHutVisuals.ts`, leaving `Huts.tsx` to compose geometry/materials from named village-system visual assets instead of owning canvas texture painting inline.
- Lazy-loaded the survival procedural world renderer itself, leaving `GameWorld.tsx` as the canvas/physics/mode shell and moving chunk streaming, terrain mounting, water, grass, scatter, and authored-village renderer orchestration into a separate survival renderer chunk.
- Lazy-loaded quest navigation beacons from the world module registry, splitting the beacon texture/animation code into a small about 2.50 kB minified / 1.14 kB gzip chunk.
- Added a scratch-capable rendered terrain color resolver to survival terrain geometry, reusing one color object per terrain/skirt build and removing per-skirt-edge color clones during chunk geometry generation.
- Added scratch-capable terrain and grass surface normal sampling, then routed high-volume BOTW grass, carpet, and flower placement probes through reusable normal targets.
- Cached rendered terrain color resolver callbacks per terrain mesh/skirt build, avoiding repeated resolver lookups during chunk geometry color upload.
- Extracted BOTW grass upload-priority ordering into `survivalBotwGrassUploadPriority.ts`, isolating viewer/lead-distance bucket sorting from the React grass renderer.
- Extracted BOTW grass completed-build caching and pending-prewarm tracking into `survivalBotwGrassBuildCache.ts`, leaving the React renderer to orchestrate build slices instead of owning cache state.
- Changed BOTW grass blade instances to store numeric RGB channels instead of one `THREE.Color` object per blade, with the instanced upload path using a single scratch color.
- Changed BOTW flower instances to store numeric leaf/bloom RGB channels instead of persistent `THREE.Color` objects, with generation and instanced upload paths reusing scratch colors.
- Removed dormant BOTW flower petal/head/center instance fields and hidden instanced meshes that were already rendered at `count = 0`, then grouped visible billboard bloom uploads in one pass with exact per-type capacities.
- Changed mountain slope grass tufts to store numeric RGB channels instead of cloned `THREE.Color` objects, with the authored mountain renderer uploading colors through one scratch color.
- Moved the mountain waterfall camera-hide check into a runtime helper so its per-frame visibility rule is isolated from the 3D mesh tree and can be tuned/tested without reopening the whole mountain renderer.
- Moved mountain mineshaft platform gap and exit-bridge frame math into a pure runtime helper, replacing inline `THREE.MathUtils` dependency in that section with lightweight numeric clamp logic.
- Moved mountain detail staging into a dedicated hook with named phase constants, keeping React timeout choreography out of the authored mountain mesh tree.
- Removed hidden ambient insect body instanced meshes and replaced the per-frame butterfly wing descriptor array with direct left/right wing uploads, keeping the live insect renderer to visible billboard wings only.
- Split the ambient insect renderer into counting and active-render children so chunks with zero butterflies/bees no longer subscribe to the frame loop.
- Converted ambient bird flocks from per-bird React mesh trees into per-species instanced body/wing/tail uploads, preserving the same flock silhouettes while reducing live mesh/material/geometric object count during exploration.
- Gated world-willow falling particle systems to the active render radius while keeping buffered willow tree silhouettes visible.
- Moved detail-scatter tree branch vectors to module-level constants so repeated biome tree renders no longer allocate fresh `THREE.Vector3` branch endpoints in JSX.
- Converted survival pond shore/water discs and lily pads from repeated mesh JSX into per-chunk instanced meshes, and changed pond/lily feature generation to direct loops instead of temporary `Array.from`/filter chains.
- Moved shared BOTW flower leaf/glow/center color constants into `survivalFoliagePalettes.ts` and reused them during flower candidate generation instead of allocating repeated target colors inside placement loops.
- Moved the active BOTW grass blade texture factory into `survivalGrassTextures.ts` and the active BOTW grass cluster geometry cache into `survivalGrassGeometry.ts`, leaving the React grass renderer without those asset factories.
- Moved BOTW grass QA dataset publishing, pending-chunk reads, prewarm/build/upload/flower counters, lead metrics, and debug-line writes into `survivalBotwGrassTelemetry.ts`.
- Moved the BOTW grass resolver contract, global resolver configuration, typed terrain/color/blocking wrappers, and surface-sample target types into `survivalBotwGrassResolvers.ts`, so `GameWorld` configures vegetation terrain inputs without importing that contract from the React renderer.
- Moved BOTW grass/flower terrain footprint sampling, slope flush limits, and restored-meadow footprint tuck rules into `survivalBotwGrassFootprints.ts`, so mountain/hill vegetation flush behavior has an isolated terrain-sampling owner.
- Removed obsolete BOTW grass fiber-line geometry generation plus the old synchronous grass/flower whole-field builders that were no longer called, leaving the sliced build pipeline as the single active vegetation generation path.
- Moved BOTW grass/carpet placement acceptance into `survivalBotwGrassPlacement.ts`, including village/base blocking, water rejection, desert bare-patch checks, restored-meadow normal relaxation, and shared blade-up orientation, so the React renderer no longer owns terrain placement policy.
- Moved deterministic BOTW grass/flower build contexts, blade and flower candidate generation, bootstrap previews, and tall feature flower insertion into `survivalBotwGrassGeneration.ts`, leaving `survivalBotwGrassRendering.tsx` focused on build scheduling, upload batching, instancing, and draw lifecycle.
- Removed stale post-extraction `GameWorld.tsx` helper copies for the unused restored-meadow terrain shader hook, dormant integrated grass color/placement path, local grass rejection summary, and orphaned terrain/grass normal wrappers, reducing the world shell from 1,202 to 934 lines.
- Moved survival procedural world QA/runtime dataset publishing and BOTW grass-warmup prewarm backoff reads into `survivalProceduralWorldTelemetry.ts`, so the chunk-stream renderer no longer owns DOM telemetry field names.
- Moved survival walk QA dataset publishing, BOTW grass-warmup reads, mana-flower collection checks, dummy-hit reads, practice-cast markers, and per-frame walk labels into `survivalWalkQaTelemetry.ts`, leaving `PlayerController.tsx` without direct QA walk browser dataset ownership.
- Moved `GameWorld` browser mode telemetry into `gameWorldTelemetry.ts` and canvas/background/light/horizon config into `gameWorldCanvasConfig.ts`, leaving the world shell closer to pure system composition.
- Moved dev fast-travel execution, perf-reset dispatch, teleport dispatch, spell-dummy range spawn dispatch, and fast-travel dataset publishing into `devFastTravel.ts`, leaving `HUD.tsx` to own only menu state and resume behavior.
- Changed HUD engine placement requests to dispatch only a placeable ID and let the placeables system validate definitions, removing the placeable catalog from the always-loaded HUD path.
- Extracted base-village water ripple hit testing, lifetime constants, next-cleanup scheduling, route-gated QA trigger checks, and direct-loop append/prune helpers into `src/game/systems/world/water/waterRippleRuntime.ts`.
- Extracted live minimap render-target sizing, render cadence, movement-threshold gating, survival-block centering, and scene hide rules into `src/game/systems/rendering/minimap/liveMiniMapRuntime.ts`.
- Extracted expanded/compact map overlay constants, full-map asset/bounds ownership, marker projection, rounded-distance labels, waypoint pinning, and village fast-travel target definitions into `src/game/ui/hud/mapOverlayRuntime.ts`.
- Moved compact-map keyboard/open gating, full-map pointer-to-waypoint solving, village fast-travel event dispatch, and expanded-map controller button/axis interpretation into `src/game/ui/hud/mapOverlayRuntime.ts`, leaving `MiniMap.tsx` focused on rendering and state wiring.
- Moved expanded-map frame sizing, editable-target keyboard guards, visible control-list assembly, focus-panel scrolling, and directional controller focus geometry into the same HUD map overlay runtime.
- Extracted static treehouse village house specs, tree placements, root collider specs, rope/bridge connection lists, span transforms, cached index ranges, and precomputed balcony/base layout into `src/game/systems/world/villages/treeHouseVillageRuntime.ts`.
- Extracted base-village campfire flicker timing, damage radius, damage rate, and point conversion helpers into `src/game/systems/world/villages/campfireRuntime.ts`.
- Extracted shield spell orientation, local/remote target-follow selection, and reusable Rapier translation objects into `src/game/systems/spells/spellShieldRuntime.ts`.
- Extracted healing crystal proximity, toxic-effect clearing, and health tick math into `src/game/systems/spells/spellHealingRuntime.ts`.

Next:
- Continue extracting survival terrain, vegetation, mountain, village, spawning, input, physics, and remaining QA logic out of the broad `GameWorld.tsx` orchestration file.
- Keep each system responsible for its own data, rendering helpers, validation, and debug hooks.

## 2. Build The Dev Game Engine Menu

Status: In progress

Done:
- Added a dev-only engine menu opened by `L`, command aliases, or a mobile DEV button.
- Added categorized spawnable objects.
- Added real rendered thumbnails that match the placeable model visuals instead of placeholder icons.
- Added shared grid-snapped placement planning behind the engine placement request path.
- Changed object cards from instant-place actions into deliberate preview/select actions, then added explicit `Place Selected`, grid-size, snap, rotate, and clear-placed controls.
- Added a world-space placement preview ghost with footprint/grid feedback, plus local placed-object persistence behind a focused storage helper.
- Added an engine placeable event bus fallback so the editor workflow does not depend on DOM `CustomEvent` availability inside embedded browser shells.
- Added a placed-object list with per-object selection, existing-object preview, move, and delete controls.
- Added editor-local placement status so placed, blocked, moved, deleted, and cleared results are readable inside the engine menu.
- Added six named placed-object save/load/delete slots backed by the focused engine placed-object storage helper.
- Added searchable placeable browsing with live category counts, compact footprint/slope/tag metadata on cards, and selected-object metadata in the placement panel.
- Moved the engine menu's catalog search/count, selection lookup, placement-option, and slot label/time rules into `engineMenuRuntime.ts`, keeping `EngineMenu.tsx` focused on rendering and event wiring.

Next:
- Add bulk placement/collection workflows once the catalog grows beyond the current starter object set.

## 3. Placeable Spawn And Placement Validation

Status: In progress

Done:
- Added terrain height and slope validation before placement.
- Added shared model/collider helpers so menu thumbnails and spawned objects use the same definitions.
- Moved editor placement request handling behind a narrow ground-height resolver so placeables no longer reach into terrain internals.
- Split placement target planning, grid snapping, slope sampling, yaw rules, and training-dummy spawn planning into a dedicated placeable rules module.
- Routed editor preview/place/clear requests through the placeables system event bus, keeping HUD clicks, terrain validation, persistence, and rendered ghost ownership isolated.
- Added footprint collision checks before preview/place requests so overlapping objects are blocked with a readable reason.
- Added existing-object move support that ignores the selected object's own footprint while still rejecting collisions with other placed objects.
- Added rotation-aware box footprints for huts and village structures, while keeping circular checks for props, nature, magic markers, and other round objects.
- Added optional multiplayer replication hooks for placed-object upsert/delete/snapshot edits, with shared sanitizers, server relay budgets, a transport-facade emit path, and remote apply events routed back through the placeables system.
- Added room-owned server placement snapshots while a multiplayer room is alive, plus a join-time snapshot handoff so late joiners can receive the current editor placement state.

Next:
- Persist/authorize placement state beyond the in-memory room lifetime only after the broader multiplayer authority model is defined.

## 4. PC, Android, And iOS HUD Scaling

Status: In progress

Done:
- Added forced mobile QA mode with `qaTouchLayout=1`.
- Added mobile DEV button support while dev mode is active.
- Added hand-position CSS nudge variables for mobile/fill/aspect-ratio cases.
- Split PC mobile-performance QA from explicit touch controls so PC test displays no longer show touchscreen buttons unless `touchControls=1` or `qaTouchControls=1` is set.
- Split forced mobile/aspect QA layout detection from touchscreen gameplay-device detection, so `qaTouchLayout=1` keeps the Android/iOS HUD layout without preserving stale PC touch gameplay buttons unless `touchControls=1` or `qaTouchControls=1` is explicitly set.
- Moved canvas resize/projection nudging into a dedicated rendering helper so aspect-ratio correction is isolated from world orchestration.
- Added app-frame QA metrics for aspect ratio, fill mode, viewport size, frame size, offsets, and insets so PC/mobile/tablet layout checks are repeatable.
- Added a PC/Android/iOS/tablet/ultrawide aspect-ratio QA profile matrix with explicit `qaAspectProfile=` overrides so layout checks are deterministic even inside the smaller in-app browser.
- Fixed forced mobile layout classification so `qaTouchLayout=1` exercises the mobile HUD layout without forcing PC touch-control buttons to render.
- Moved mobile layout defaults, touch-layout class synchronization, and app viewport CSS variable writing into the input system so PC/mobile HUD scaling behavior has one runtime owner.
- Moved app-frame aspect-ratio sizing into a dedicated UI helper so frame math is isolated from App orchestration.
- Added a route-gated HUD layout QA probe for the aspect-ratio matrix so PC/mobile routes can report expected HUD pieces, missing pieces, frame clipping, overlap pairs, frame size, and compact rect telemetry for minimap, spell/settings menus, touch controls, status HUD, mana, and hand layers.
- Added a lazy-minimap mounted marker so HUD layout QA expects compact/expanded map targets only after the app-frame minimap chunk is actually mounted.
- Added DEV-only `qaHudState=` routes for spell menu, settings panel, engine menu, expanded map, scoreboard, quest NPC editor, and forced magic-hands states so HUD layout can be forced and measured without manual clicking.
- Added `qaHudState=inventory` to the forced HUD route matrix and gave the inventory panel direct layout telemetry for PC/mobile aspect checks.
- Moved the `qaHudState=` route-forcing runtime into the QA tools boundary so HUD layout automation is isolated from ordinary HUD rendering logic.
- Added a shared aspect-ratio HUD route matrix that combines named device profiles with gameplay, spell, settings, engine, expanded-map, scoreboard, quest NPC editor, and forced magic-hands states, then publishes the state cases and generated routes through app-frame QA datasets.
- Added a local QA canvas resize fallback path so PC/Android/iOS HUD routes do not randomly fail visual checks because the embedded browser skipped the first ResizeObserver notification.
- Added QA telemetry for the actual WebGL canvas CSS/backing size so aspect-ratio checks can catch wrapper-vs-canvas mismatches.
- Added snapshot-guarded app-frame QA metric publishing so unchanged frame/profile measurements do not rewrite browser datasets every render tick.
- Fixed the 844x390 short-landscape magic-hands offset so forced mobile HUD routes keep the arm layer inside the app frame instead of clipping above the viewport.
- Re-checked the engine menu catalog/search surface on desktop and forced touch-layout routes after the metadata pass; the embedded browser production build exposed app-frame metrics and visible DOM checks but not DEV-only HUD layout datasets.
- Re-checked the spell menu on desktop and forced touch-layout routes after the readability pass; desktop stayed at `1280x720` with no horizontal overflow, and the touch-layout fallback now auto-fits spell cards instead of stretching them across the PC test shell.
- Added direct `qaMapPage=live|world` HUD route forcing so expanded-map QA can verify the local and full-map tabs without brittle click automation.
- Re-checked the expanded map on desktop live/world routes and the forced Android portrait/touch-layout world-map route; the modal stayed inside the `1280x720` app frame, had 0 horizontal overflow, avoided topbar/tabs/close/status overlap, hid gameplay touch buttons, and published the requested `android-portrait` aspect-matrix profile.
- Tightened magic-hand HUD fit by measuring the visible left/right hand masks instead of oversized animation frames and keeping PC/touch Fill-mode hand offsets inside the app frame.
- Reworked mobile joystick normalization, Magic Glass Orb hand-effect line length, and QA walk waypoint progress distance checks to use explicit squared-distance square roots, keeping active HUD/input/player paths free of `Math.hypot`.

Next:
- Run the matrix on real PC, Android, and iOS hardware once the browser test routes are stable.

- Moved HUD directional focus and settings-panel index metadata into focused helpers so settings/menu readability work can tune layout and navigation without growing the HUD shell.
- Moved the reusable magic-hand sprite canvas into a focused HUD component so arm sizing/asset fallback work is isolated from spell-effect rendering.
- Moved magic-hand pose timing into a dedicated hook so arm clipping/aspect-ratio tuning can happen without touching individual spell effect canvases.
- Moved magic-hand spell-effect rendering into a focused HUD component so spell menu/readability work can tune held-spell visuals without expanding the hand shell.
- Moved magic-hand effect pixel cleanup, glass-orb signal math, and tornado pixel-block data into a focused runtime helper so arm/effect polish can tune rendering without mixing in reusable targeting and sprite cleanup logic.
- Moved lazy HUD panel registration into `hudLazyModules.ts` so spell, map, quest, inventory, engine, fast-travel, name, score, mobile, and fullscreen surfaces have one optional-module registry.
- Moved the settings panel into its own lazy HUD module so settings readability and PC/mobile aspect fixes can iterate without expanding the always-loaded HUD shell.
- Moved the live character preview renderer into its own HUD component so the character menu can be polished independently from the always-loaded gameplay HUD.
- Moved spell-menu filtering/count/thumbnail derivation into a focused runtime helper so readability work can tune the catalog renderer without mixing it into spell data ownership.
- Moved inventory entry, slot-grid, quest status, and Darrel progress derivation into a focused runtime helper so the survival pack can keep improving on PC/mobile without mixing quest rules into modal rendering.
- Moved the survival-pack wizard preview canvas into its own HUD component so modal layout polish and avatar preview drawing can iterate independently.
- Moved settings focus/default/label/color derivation into a focused runtime helper so video, keybinds, voice, and character settings can keep improving without burying device/layout rules inside the renderer.
- Moved quest NPC editor role lists, scriptpoint duplication/reordering/removal, event builder line generation, draft cleanup, and preview summaries into a focused runtime helper so the editor can be polished without expanding the QuestPanels renderer.
- Added a dedicated launch UI boundary for the press/save/new/multiplayer flow and lazy launch avatar preview.
- Moved the launch menu renderer out of `App.tsx` so the start/save/new/multiplayer screens can be polished independently from the app frame.
- Tightened the inventory panel's phone layout so 9-slot backpack and quick-row grids fit the modal without inner horizontal scrolling.
- Improved the engine menu catalog surface with search, live category counts, compact tag/footprint metadata, empty search feedback, and selected-object details.
- Improved the spell menu with family filters, visible spell counts, binding/left/right status strips, per-card family/current metadata, and desktop/touch responsive catalog grids while preserving the existing hotbar binding behavior.
- Improved the expanded map with a responsive topbar, compact page tabs, safer full-map/live-map frame sizing, route-selectable map pages, compressed world-map status/village controls, and compact minimap compass/player marker sizing.
- Improved the command console with a runtime-owned suggestion catalog, touch-friendly command chips, safer app-viewport sizing, and clearer input text.
- Improved the settings panel with shared readability class hooks, larger tab/card/control text floors, two-column tab wrapping on narrow panels, and mobile overrides that no longer force 7px tabs.
- Improved the pause/start/lobby menu surface with shared readable text variables, class-owned title/button/rule/invite sizing, mobile overrides that protect labels from the broad overlay button rule, and normalized letter spacing on menu controls.

Next:
- Run real PC/Android/iOS visual passes once the built-in browser path is available again.

## 6. Optimization And Smoothness

Status: In progress

Decision:
- The world stays huge. Optimization does not mean shrinking the map or removing the open-world exploration goal.
- Avoid lazy-loading core gameplay systems unless we explicitly decide otherwise.
- Useful streaming/culling later means keeping distant chunks, decorations, models, and nonessential UI work from wasting frame time while preserving the open-world experience.

Done:
- Identified that `GameWorld.tsx` is still doing too much orchestration.
- Kept real thumbnail previews on demand instead of continuously animating them.
- Moved engine/editor placement logic out of `GameWorld.tsx`.
- Code-split the dev engine menu so normal gameplay HUD startup does not eagerly load that optional tool UI.
- Tightened controller-vs-touch input routing so controller confirm/resume does not enter the touch fullscreen flow on PC QA runs.
- Changed primary-gamepad selection to scan connected pads directly instead of allocating/filtering/sorting every controller poll, while preserving active/preferred/standard/fallback priority.
- Added a sub-frame primary-gamepad scan cache so HUD, player, voice, launch, map, and modal controller polling can share the same selected pad result instead of rescanning the browser gamepad list multiple times inside one visual frame.
- Changed movement-key reset, number-slot parsing, and controller release checks to use stable direct loops instead of object-key arrays, regex matches, and per-check `Set` allocation.
- Moved touch fullscreen runtime into the input system, keeping fullscreen state subscriptions and request/hint behavior out of the HUD render body.
- Moved canvas ResizeObserver fallback installation and explicit R3F canvas resize polyfill into the rendering system, fixing local QA routes that stalled at a 300x150 canvas until a later resize.
- Added rendering-owned sizing for the actual WebGL canvas element plus QA CSS/backing-size telemetry, fixing the R3F wrapper/full-canvas mismatch seen in embedded PC visual checks.
- Moved pure settings-panel index math and directional menu focus solving out of `HUD.tsx`, reducing HUD runtime surface before the larger settings/menu readability pass.
- Moved shared HUD/launch directional menu focus solving into `src/game/ui/menu/menuNavigation.ts` with direct candidate scoring, removing duplicate candidate-array/sort chains from controller/keyboard menu navigation.

- Moved magic-hand pose timing and aspect-offset calculation out of `MagicHands.tsx`, reducing per-frame HUD renderer complexity around the arm layer.
- Moved held-spell HUD effects out of `MagicHands.tsx`; the hand shell now delegates fireball/ice/heal/rings/lightning/GIF/shield/kunai/orb/buff effect rendering through `MagicHandSpellEffects.tsx`.
- Moved magic-hand spell-effect pixel cleanup, magic-glass-orb signal solving, and tiny tornado block generation into `magicHandSpellEffectsRuntime.ts`, and added `qaHudState=magichands&qaMagicSpell=fireball` so PC/mobile hand-effect clipping can be forced through the normal QA routes.
- Moved the GIF-based hand overlays for Blink, Smoke Bomb, and Portal into `magicHandGifSpellEffects.tsx`, making spell-effect visuals easier to split and verify family-by-family.
