- Moved staged survival scatter orchestration into the vegetation system, leaving `GameWorld.tsx` to provide only grass-system, terrain surface-quality, terrain-height, and water-level inputs for solid trees, detail props, huts, wildflowers, insects, underbrush, rocks, desert landmarks, and birds.
- Gated the 10-sample BOTW grass debug line to grass inspection mode so ordinary perf QA routes avoid extra per-second terrain/grass sampling.
- Moved active BOTW-style survival grass rendering/build/upload/prewarm/debug sampling into the vegetation system; `GameWorld.tsx` now supplies only narrow terrain/color/blocking/water/debug resolvers and mounts the field.
- Moved disabled local/tutorial grass renderers out of the live world path, keeping the archived code type-checked in vegetation while removing two dormant field mounts from normal survival gameplay.
- Moved disabled chunk-level ground grass cover/patch renderers into the dormant vegetation archive and removed stale grass config/texture/geometry/shader/instancing imports from `GameWorld.tsx`.
- Added `network/gameNetworkClient.ts` and moved HUD, hands, player movement, projectiles, and runes off direct Socket.IO access so gameplay code now uses a narrow local-identity/event-emission boundary.
- Moved engine placement planning and grid snapping out of the placed-object renderer so future preview/save/network paths can reuse one placement rule system.
- Moved self-contained HUD panels out of the monolithic HUD shell to reduce coupling before larger menu optimization passes.
- Lazy-loaded the player-name entry prompt so normal gameplay avoids loading that lobby-only portal UI.
- Moved invite room-code helpers out of the HUD shell so lobby/network utilities are reusable without coupling to visual UI.
- Moved App launch-room handling onto the networking invite helper, removing duplicate parser/URL/mobile-invite behavior from the app shell.
- Moved launch-menu helpers/config out of `App.tsx` and lazy-loaded the launch avatar preview; normal gameplay and the first launch screen no longer load the preview canvas renderer.
- Moved app-frame/aspect-ratio QA metric writing out of `App.tsx`, leaving App to schedule frame updates while QA owns the dataset contract and profile matrix integration.
- Moved survival QA observer bootstrapping out of `App.tsx`, so QA routes own their auto-launch behavior and App only invokes the helper.
- Moved controller settings metadata out of `HUD.tsx`, keeping the HUD focused on rendering and menu state while the input system owns button/action definitions.
- Moved static character customization menu data out of `HUD.tsx`, shrinking the renderer's configuration footprint.
- Moved keyboard keybind guide metadata out of `HUD.tsx`, keeping settings/help text beside the input system.
- Moved reusable HUD settings helpers out of `HUD.tsx`, keeping repeated menu indexing/formatting/color-validation logic isolated from the render tree.
- Moved repeated editable-target shortcut checks out of HUD, player movement, inventory, quest panels, and voice chat into the input system so typing protection stays consistent across PC/mobile/controller UI.
- Moved browser display and pointer-lock helper logic out of `HUD.tsx`, keeping fullscreen/pointer-lock edge cases inside the input/platform boundary.
- Lazy-loaded the character customization live preview so normal gameplay avoids loading that settings-only avatar canvas renderer.
- Added the repeatable aspect-ratio QA matrix and named profile override path, keeping PC/mobile layout checks measurable while preserving open-world gameplay loading.
- Moved App mobile layout defaults, touch-layout class writes, and app viewport CSS variable updates into the input runtime, reducing App shell platform logic while preserving PC/mobile QA behavior.
- Moved the launch menu renderer out of `App.tsx` and into its own lazy UI chunk, reducing the App shell while preserving the first-screen and direct-survival paths.
- Moved App frame sizing math into `appFrameLayout.ts`, plus moved viewport listener installation and browser zoom prevention into the input runtime, leaving App with narrower setup effects.
- Moved App's lazy gameplay/HUD/launch/minimap module registry, idle preload scheduler, and app error boundary into `src/game/ui/appFrame/`, leaving App as a smaller composition layer.
- Moved spell dummy QA helper logic out of `Projectiles.tsx` and made the dummy test targets snapshot-driven visuals instead of Rapier bodies, avoiding physics-world teardown errors on the QA route while preserving F9 hit telemetry.
- Gated the active spell dummy QA component behind `qaSpellDummies=1` so normal gameplay avoids dummy-only state/effect hooks and the dummy Canvas commit path.
- Moved status spell config/effect metadata and projectile texture/cache preloading out of `Projectiles.tsx`, reducing spell-renderer ownership while preserving eager core projectile visuals.
- Moved projectile tuning and pure math helpers out of `Projectiles.tsx`, leaving the renderer with fewer cross-system constants and less non-React utility code.
- Moved procedural tornado/meteor pixel sprite texture caching out of `Projectiles.tsx`, so expensive canvas visual factories live beside other spell visual asset helpers.
- Moved projectile ownership checks behind named spell helpers, reducing repeated raw network-player ID comparisons in projectile components.
- Moved the active spell dummy QA scene out of `Projectiles.tsx`, so normal projectile rendering no longer contains dummy state, respawn timers, QA key sequencing, or dummy label markup.
- Moved portal/blink traversal out of `Projectiles.tsx`, dropping the renderer to about 2,000 lines and moving the portal store subscription into `ActivePortals`.
- Moved disc/orb shield rendering out of `Projectiles.tsx`, dropping the renderer to about 1,784 lines and keeping defensive shader/collider logic in its own spell module.
- Moved grab control and arm rendering out of `Projectiles.tsx`, dropping the renderer to about 1,466 lines while keeping player pull/hold logic inside its own spell module.
- Moved area, smoke, and healing spell effects out of `Projectiles.tsx`, dropping the renderer to about 849 lines and keeping long-lived effect loops inside focused spell modules.
- Moved the remaining direct spell renderers out of `Projectiles.tsx`, dropping it to about 60 lines so the component now only routes active projectiles to owning spell modules.
- Moved global movement key state, mouse gameplay fallback helpers, meditation/slot key helpers, controller release readiness, and touch-button typing into `playerInputState.ts`, reducing `PlayerController.tsx` input ownership.
- Moved survival player spawn override resolution, cached QA route snapshot parsing, temporary village spawn routing, random survival spawn selection, and base-village QA safe-height clamping into `survivalPlayerSpawn.ts`, reducing `PlayerController.tsx` spawn ownership.
- Kept the authored base chunk in the survival stream as a placeholder while `SurvivalChunk` returns null for `0:0` when the base village owns terrain/collision, fixing the base-center Rapier cleanup crash.
- Moved HUD command-console command parsing/dispatch into `hudCommandConsole.ts`, isolating dev commands, navigation recording commands, Darrel spawn commands, and time-of-day commands from the HUD renderer.
- Moved the always-visible gameplay mana/status/hotbar overlay into `GameplayHudOverlay.tsx` without lazy-loading it, keeping core HUD responsive while isolating aspect-ratio/status layout work.
- Moved player scoreboard row/status shaping into `playerScoreRows.ts`, keeping multiplayer roster data prep beside the score UI instead of in the HUD shell.
- Changed scoreboard row/status shaping to use direct status assembly and one sorted remote-player list, avoiding status/filter/map/spread chains before 32-player roster work.
- Moved Lily Coil tube path constants, frame sampling, nearest-surface solving, and active chunk gating into `lilyCoilTubeMotion.ts`, leaving `PlayerController.tsx` to apply player movement rather than own realm geometry math.
- Moved survival walk QA route selection, tuning constants, intent/dummy/mana/quest readers, and player-position QA dataset publishing into `survivalWalkQa.ts`, dropping `PlayerController.tsx` to about 4,033 lines and keeping soak-test behavior out of core movement ownership.
- Added a dedicated player-controller system boundary and moved collider, movement speed, ground probe, floor recovery, camera anti-clip, ladder/vclip, grab, and self-buff tuning into `src/game/systems/player/playerMovementConfig.ts`.
- Moved player camera roll detection, Lily Coil camera reset, and mouse/controller/tube look-delta application into `src/game/systems/player/playerCameraRuntime.ts`, leaving `PlayerController.tsx` to invoke camera behavior instead of owning the math.
- Moved player spell launch placement and remote-target selection into `playerSpellCasting.ts`, further separating combat math from the player movement/camera loop.
- Moved player camera wall/terrain anti-clip collision sampling into `playerCameraRuntime.ts`, keeping Rapier body mutation in the controller while the player camera system owns the projection/raycast decision rules.
- Reused `playerSpellCasting.ts` for QA practice casts and flamethrower ticks, removing duplicate hand-offset/spawn-origin math from the per-frame controller loop.
- Moved player ground probing and floor/deep-floor recovery target solving into `playerGroundingRuntime.ts`, removing Rapier ray construction loops from the controller while preserving controller ownership of body/camera side effects.
- Moved controller spell-casting and controller hotbar polling into `playerControllerCasting.ts`, keeping face-button/menu polling in HUD and cast-button behavior in the player-controller system.
- Moved survival walk QA runtime refs and reset/session setup out of `PlayerController.tsx`, dropping the controller from about 3,277 lines to about 3,211 lines and keeping automated soak-test bookkeeping inside the QA tools boundary.
- Moved regular player controller runtime refs and scratch vectors into `playerControllerRuntimeState.ts`, dropping `PlayerController.tsx` to about 3,198 lines while avoiding fresh movement vector allocations on controller re-renders.
- Moved player screen-shake event parsing, expiry reset, and camera offset math into `playerScreenShakeRuntime.ts`, leaving `PlayerController.tsx` to pass camera, frame epoch, and reusable vector scratch into the player-system boundary.
- Moved player toxic status damage calculation, local damage buffering, and network sync decision timing into `playerToxicDamageRuntime.ts`, leaving `PlayerController.tsx` to apply health and emit any buffered sync payload.
- Moved camera anti-clip body correction into `playerCameraRuntime.ts` and added a stable player solid-collider filter shared by QA probes, ground checks, floor recovery, and camera anti-clip instead of recreating collider predicates every frame.
- Moved floor recovery body/camera/event application into `playerGroundingRuntime.ts`, keeping the controller responsible for the recovery condition while the grounding system owns the recovery write sequence.
- Gated navigation recorder payload construction behind `isNavigationRecordingActive()` in normal movement and Lily Coil traversal, so ordinary gameplay no longer builds navigation sample arrays before the recorder can early-return.
- Reused named player-owned scratch vectors for grabbed-player follow math, meditation camera targets, screen shake, QA walk position/yaw checks, QA practice/flamethrower cast directions, Lily Coil camera traversal, and navigation samples, avoiding avoidable hot-path `Vector3` allocations while keeping network/event payloads copied.
- Added scratch-capable player spell launch helpers and routed controller spell/grab/QA/flamethrower launch paths through them with copied payload positions, reducing repeated combat-path vector/clone allocations.
- Reused direct-status targeting scratch vectors plus squared miss-distance checks, and moved local player network pose aim reads onto stable network scratch vectors.
- Added reusable Lily Coil tube frame and nearest-surface scratch helpers, then moved QA tube route sampling and live tube traversal onto `getLilyCoilTubeFrameInto(...)` so the walk-bot and active tube movement avoid repeated frame/vector allocation.
- Added reusable player camera look scratch for Lily Coil camera reset/look-delta math, removing fresh look vectors and quaternions from the tube-active input path while preserving the existing camera behavior.
- Reused spell renderer scratch vectors/quaternions for Phase Beam camera locking, beam transforms, spawn hit projection, Kunai line endpoints, and Rings of Power spawn velocity; replaced Healing Crystal per-frame vector distance checks with squared-distance math.
- Added scratch-capable projectile aim and cylinder-placement helpers, moved Grab Spell aim-cone checks, local hit projection, arm path points, palm/finger placement, and player grab-follow aim updates onto reusable vectors/arrays.
- Reused live-minimap render-pass scratch arrays and cached-player snapshots, switched campfire damage proximity to squared-distance math, and moved Grab Spell arm path vectors into stable memoized storage.
- Folded Lily Coil nearest-surface return data into its reusable scratch object and gave projectile/camera helper defaults stable scratch fallbacks so future callers do not quietly reintroduce helper-level allocations.
- Reused astral veil and Lily Coil foreground meadow frame vectors, removing camera-facing overlay allocations from active visual effects.
- Replaced Kunai tether line `setFromPoints` frame allocations with a stable position buffer, reused its collision pull vector, and memoized shield orientation/uniform objects.
- Memoized DEV HUD route parameters and collapsed the buff-clock active-status check to one timestamp per effect run, reducing avoidable HUD render work.
- Tightened QA quest-spawn and chunk-stream initialization so descriptive run labels cannot accidentally teleport a route away from its explicit `qaSurvivalChunk`.
- Made `qaSpellDummies=1` a first-class survival QA route, including auto-launch and deterministic dummy-range spawn ownership.
- Gated the spell-dummy QA scene until after `Canvas.onCreated`, so QA dummy meshes and Drei labels cannot participate in initial renderer creation.
- Added a narrow app-frame guard for known Rapier rigid-body teardown errors so PC/mobile QA route switches and settings reloads do not leave the player on the red crash screen.
- Moved raw survival terrain height, smoothed/rendered terrain color, village pad/base/skirt wrappers, and terrain/mana resolver setup out of `GameWorld.tsx` into `survivalTerrainSurface.ts`.
- Moved survival grass surface height/normal sampling, mountain/village grass overrides, unified surface samples, water/village grass blocking, BOTW grass resolver setup, and debug sampling out of `GameWorld.tsx` into `survivalGrassSurface.ts`, dropping the world scene shell to about 178 lines.
- Added scratch-capable player spell launch and targeting helpers, reused controller spell/grab launch scratch for repeated QA/flamethrower/combat paths, removed a Lily Coil network aim clone, and moved player network pose sync onto reusable aim-vector scratch.
- Moved HUD touch gameplay runtime effects into `useHudTouchGameplayRuntime.ts`, keeping touch resize detection, forced mobile QA activation, PC touch cleanup, and CSS-class synchronization out of the always-growing HUD shell.
- Moved active gameplay buff countdown timing into `useHudBuffClock.ts` and the gameplay overlay, reducing the parent HUD's active-buff refresh from 250ms to a scoreboard-only 1000ms cadence while preserving fast poison/acid bar updates inside the overlay.
- Removed spell dummy hitscan/area-hit vector allocations, allowed dummy area-hit checks to consume plain `{x,y,z}` centers, switched IceSpell proximity to squared numeric distance, and avoided per-sample mountain terrain palette/color/normal allocations during mountain terrain and slope-grass builds.
- Moved graveyard terrain palette/color sampling onto scratch colors and changed cached survival/base/village-pad terrain color uploads to pre-sized typed buffers, reducing chunk-build churn during open-world streaming and authored village mounting.
- Preallocated survival terrain skirt position/color/index buffers in `survivalTerrainGeometry.ts`, removing push-grown skirt arrays from core chunk seam geometry.
- Changed active BOTW flower instances to store numeric leaf/bloom RGB channels with scratch-backed generation and upload, matching the blade instance storage cleanup and reducing vegetation build-cache object weight.
- Removed the inactive BOTW flower petal/head/center mesh path, skipped its per-flower matrix/color upload work, grouped visible billboard bloom types in one pass, and sized bloom instanced-mesh capacities to actual counts instead of 3x overcapacity.
- Changed authored mountain slope grass tuft storage to numeric RGB channels and reused one scratch color during instanced color uploads, removing per-tuft `THREE.Color` clones from mountain grass generation.
- Trimmed the live ambient insect renderer to visible billboard wing meshes only and removed per-frame wing descriptor allocation, preserving animated butterflies/bees while cutting invisible draw/update work.
- Converted ambient bird rendering to per-species instanced meshes and changed flock generation from `Array.from` callbacks to a direct loop, reducing React tree size and generated closure churn for ordinary wilderness chunks.
- Hoisted detail-scatter branch endpoint vectors out of the render path for jungle, swamp, and default biome trees, keeping tree visuals stable while avoiding repeated branch `Vector3` allocations.
- Instanced pond shore/water discs and lily pads in the survival water renderer, reducing streamed water-feature mesh trees while preserving aggregate pond/lily QA counters on grassland and swamp routes.
- Instanced rock outcrops by boulder/spire type with per-instance colors, reducing streamed wilderness mesh/material object count while preserving aggregate rock QA counts.
- Changed aggregate survival QA feature counter publishing to direct `Map` iteration, removing temporary arrays and reducer objects when streamed feature counts update.
- Changed bush and fern underbrush instanced uploads from one pass per palette color to one pass per feature list, and removed unused per-bush key strings from generated blobs.
- Changed solid-tree grove spacing checks to squared-distance loops and tree instanced uploads to one pass per tree list instead of rescanning the grove for each geometry variant.
- Moved world-willow particle count/bounds setup out of the per-frame animation loop, keeping each frame to matrix uploads only, and changed willow generation/visibility helpers to direct loops.
- Changed `qaPerfStats=1` frame sampling to a fixed ring buffer, removing per-frame `shift()` reindexing and replacing once-per-second reducer/filter passes with direct sample loops.
- Cached static aspect-ratio QA profile/state/route summaries and changed closest-profile matching plus route generation to direct loops, reducing repeated QA string generation while keeping PC/Android/iOS layout checks deterministic.
- Reduced the non-touch PC `Fill` magic-hand offset so the hands stay low without pushing the measured HUD layer beyond the app frame on 16:9 gameplay routes.
- Changed HUD layout QA publishing to use static target descriptors and direct expected/measured/failure assembly, avoiding repeated target arrays, filters, maps, spreads, and `Map` snapshots during aspect-route measurement ticks.
- Reused the QA perf probe's sorted-sample scratch array across publish ticks so perf-stat routes stop allocating a fresh array every second.
- Changed the canvas resize nudge to apply WebGL size/projection updates only when parent size or device-pixel-ratio changes, publishing apply/skip QA counters for duplicate resize callbacks.
- Changed HUD layout QA publishing to compare a stable layout signature before rewriting the full expected/measured/failure dataset, leaving small publish/skip counters for aspect-route verification.
- Changed magic-hand pose frame cycling from an interval to a self-scheduled timeout loop, preserving the 200ms hand animation cadence while avoiding a persistent interval handle.
- Added a shared self-scheduled looped-frame HUD hook and moved character preview, inventory wizard preview, magic-hand sprite idle frames, and lightning hand idle frames off fixed intervals.
- Changed the QA perf probe's stalled-RAF fallback from a fixed interval to a self-scheduled timeout while leaving normal RAF telemetry unchanged.
- Replaced app-frame aspect string parsing/style reconstruction with prebuilt static layouts for `16/9`, `4/3`, `21/9`, and `Fill`, keeping aspect switching deterministic while removing per-render frame-style churn.
- Added a scoreboard-specific HUD QA target and touch-layout roster compaction so PC, Android, and iOS checks can measure the roster panel directly without relying on manual Tab/controller holds.
- Moved network player-id key assembly into the networking facade and changed room-state handling, status-effect clearing, voice peer scans, grab sync, and controller hotbar resets to direct loops instead of array/filter/reducer chains.
- Changed survival chunk reconciliation, local/tutorial grass stream reconciliation, and procedural stream prewarm/telemetry loops to use direct loops and squared-distance gates before more expensive distance work.
- Changed BOTW grass generation, carpet checks, upload-priority bucketing, and active frame-loop recenter/prewarm state to use direct loops, stable scratch refs, and squared-distance checks instead of temporary arrays, `Set` creation, object replacement, and avoidable `Math.hypot` calls.
- Changed survival biome weighting, restored-meadow masks, water-level blending, strict-desert checks, grass-surface village/base blocking, and desert grass-biome fallback to avoid hot-path maps, filters, sorts, reducers, override string keys, and unnecessary square roots.
- Changed QA walk dummy/mana/quest targeting plus dummy/mana snapshot readers to use single-pass direct scans and squared-distance thresholds, keeping the automated soak route from adding avoidable allocation noise while it tests gameplay.
- Changed detail scatter spacing, ambient insect wing updates, solid-tree final mesh sync, town-route masks, survival/base terrain band checks, graveyard approach scans, mountain grass blocking, slope-grass radial checks, and BOTW terrain footprints to use direct loops, squared gates, reused scratch objects, or unrolled samples.
- Changed the player controller's hand/casting, QA escape/turn, dummy targeting, movement telemetry, progress, recovery, and low-speed checks to avoid per-frame temporary hand arrays and unnecessary square roots while preserving exact distance where progress deltas require it.
- Changed spell target acquisition, direct beam hit checks, grab targeting, spell-dummy hitscan/area publishing, area-effect animation loops, and meteor impact checks to use direct scans and squared gates before exact distance work.
- Changed survival mana-source safe-zone checks, random reserved-spawn chunk checks, QA chunk URL parsing, QA spawn key formatting, and terrain-detail biome-weight summing to avoid repeated temporary arrays and avoidable square roots during spawn/chunk setup.
- Changed voice-chat proximity volume to reject out-of-range peers with squared distance before exact falloff math, preserving smooth proximity audio while reducing interval work for larger rooms.
- Changed voice soundboard blips, soundboard debug summaries, and remote-peer proximity volume refresh from fixed intervals to cancellable timeout chains while preserving their previous cadences.
- Changed HUD magic-hand healing signal scans and touch gameplay release cleanup to use direct loops and a single final distance calculation, preserving HUD QA fit behavior.
- Moved mana recharge runtime bookkeeping out of `Runes.tsx`, replaced the 15-second rune shuffle sort with a partial Fisher-Yates pick, changed mana-flower QA publishing/cooldown cleanup to direct loops, and changed live flower/well pickup checks from `Math.hypot` to squared-distance gates.
- Fixed survival mana flowers getting stuck at zero when `Runes.tsx` queried the source generator before terrain resolvers were configured; the component now waits for the survival mana-source boundary before caching the current chunk's source list.
- Changed base-village rune-source cycling from a fixed 15-second interval to a self-scheduled timeout that only stays alive while base rune sources are visible.
- Changed active rune-power decay from a fixed HUD interval to a cancellable one-shot timeout chain, preserving the one-second decay cadence while sleeping whenever gameplay is inactive or both hands are empty.
- Moved survival terrain/grass/water resolver ownership behind the lazy survival renderer and moved engine/base ground-height lookups behind their lazy systems, reducing the main `GameWorld` production chunk from about `2,414.71` kB / `894.10` kB gzip to about `2,369.65` kB / `876.26` kB gzip without lazy-loading the core player controller.
- Changed active BOTW grass flower grouping/upload, wildflower upload, solid-tree setup, dormant tree dressing spacing/upload, tornado/meteor setup, smoke-cloud updates, and pixel spell sprite uploads to use direct loops, prebuilt instance boxes, reusable colors/vectors, and fewer setup-time callbacks while preserving the same survival and spell visuals.
- Moved base-village ripple state cleanup off the per-frame `useFrame` scan and onto a next-expiry timeout, keeping per-frame work limited to the visual ring fade while the water runtime owns squared-radius hit bands and direct-loop pruning.
- Moved live minimap render-gating policy into a pure rendering helper so compact/expanded cadence, movement threshold checks, and block-centered camera placement can be tuned without touching the Three.js HUD component.
- Moved full-map and compact marker math into a shared HUD map helper, switching waypoint pin checks to squared-radius gates and centralizing rounded distance labels so expanded map rendering stays mostly presentational.
- Moved expanded-map controller focus scoring and visible-control derivation out of the render component, preserving one focus ring and avoiding repeated geometry/focus logic inside `MiniMap.tsx`.
- Reworked `TreeHouseVillage.tsx` to reuse cached stair/rung index ranges, precomputed balcony/tree-base vectors, shared rope/bridge connection lists, and direct span math instead of render-time `Array.from`, `distanceTo`, `Math.pow`, and repeated vector cloning.
- Replaced campfire per-frame random light flicker with a deterministic smooth flicker helper and routed proximity damage through a shared squared-distance radius check with one game-store read.
- Moved disc/orb shield orientation and player-follow math out of the render component, reusing vector/quaternion/euler scratch plus stable `{x,y,z}` translation objects instead of allocating helper objects during shield updates.
- Switched tornado, meteor shower, and phase-beam frame fades from repeated wall-clock reads to the R3F render clock, and tightened spell-dummy QA timestamps to one timestamp per hit/cast/respawn tick.
- Moved healing crystal radius, toxic clearing, and health tick calculation into a pure runtime helper so the frame loop uses one store read and one squared-distance decision.
- Moved survival QA observer bootstrapping out of the `App.tsx` render path and into layout/effect hooks, preserving route auto-launch while keeping app-frame render pure.
- Moved DOM minimap player-position tracking into `useMiniMapPlayerTracking`, added shared modal-blocking rules for the DOM and live Three.js minimaps, and stopped hidden map overlays from scheduling visible state/icon updates while other HUD modals are open.
- Tightened the lazy magic-hands mount gate to require visible armed hands, and paused the hand pose frame interval defensively whenever magic hands are not animating.
- Added a HUD tool-overlay map suppression contract so engine/dev/quest overlays can hide app-level minimap DOM surfaces and stop minimap tracking updates without moving local tool state into the global store.
- Changed mana-flower cooldown pruning from an always-on one-second interval to a next-expiry timeout, so the rune/mana system sleeps when no flowers are cooling down.
- Changed engine-menu and dev-fast-travel opening to close any expanded-map state before showing the tool overlay, and tightened HUD QA map expectations so hidden tool overlays cannot leave invisible map work alive.
- Moved lobby notification expiry math into `hudLobbyMessageRuntime.ts` and replaced the always-on one-second cleanup interval with a next-expiry timeout while messages are visible.
- Gated the 120 ms mouse-gameplay resume reconciliation loop so it only keeps polling while pointer lock and HUD/input state are out of sync, instead of running throughout normal mouse gameplay.
- Changed mouse-gameplay resume reconciliation from a gated fixed interval to a self-scheduled timeout chain, so each mismatch check decides whether another wakeup is still needed.
- Changed survival autosave from a fixed HUD interval to a cancellable one-shot timeout chain that preserves the 15-second save cadence while survival gameplay is active.
- Gated `CanvasRuntimeProbe` frame-count dataset writes behind QA/perf route flags, so ordinary gameplay keeps only the canvas-mounted marker instead of writing repeated DOM frame telemetry.
- Gated base-village bonfire/hut rune sources behind a mana-runtime visibility helper so ordinary wilderness survival keeps mana flowers/wells active without running the base rune shuffle interval or hidden base spawner.
- Replaced survival visible-chunk staging's fixed interval with a self-scheduling timeout/background-task chain that only schedules the next mount step when reconciliation adds or upgrades visible chunk work.
- Gated BOTW grass lead/speed/viewer-distance DOM metrics behind QA/perf/walk/debug route flags while keeping upload progress and upload ratio snapshots available for readiness checks.
- Cached the BOTW grass debug-view route flag at field mount time so normal gameplay no longer checks `window.location.search` inside the grass frame loop while `qaGrassView=1` still publishes the debug sample line.
- Moved rune-power decay decisions into the mana runtime, made the active-control HUD timer sleep while both hands are empty, and made same-value rune-power store writes no-op so empty-hand gameplay does not re-render the HUD every second.
- Moved survival procedural pending-chunk reads into an in-memory telemetry accessor so active BOTW grass recenter delay no longer reads browser datasets every frame while QA counters still publish to the DOM.
- Time-sliced survival mana-source reconciliation to a 250 ms cadence and moved mana-flower pickup cooldown checks onto a render-clock epoch helper, reducing frame-loop wall-clock reads while keeping deterministic mana source generation intact.
- Cached QA day/night route overrides at sky and terrain component mount time so terrain tint and sky frame loops no longer parse `window.location.search` across every visible chunk every frame.
- Reused one player-controller frame timestamp for grab, astral, Lily Coil, slide, and network timing, and cached QA-walk route flags outside the frame loop so the controller no longer reparses route state during movement ticks.
- Moved live-minimap render cadence and map-toggle delay checks onto the Three.js render clock, and made first-render readiness explicit so the minimap stays responsive without `performance.now()` reads in its frame path.
- Moved visible villager look-update cadence onto the Three.js render clock and cached villager reaction counts so NPC frame loops no longer multiply browser clock reads or allocate `Object.keys(...)` arrays when no one is reacting.
- Centralized active water-ripple animation timing behind one shared ripple frame-clock component and passed the mobile/desktop segment count from the parent, avoiding per-ripple wall-clock reads and device-mode checks while ripples overlap.
- Changed remote-player status visuals from per-player 250 ms intervals to next-expiry timeouts through a dedicated network runtime helper, so remote avatars sleep between actual status transitions.
- Replaced the HUD buff/status countdown interval with a self-scheduled timeout helper that wakes at visible second changes or expiry edges, keeping gameplay and scoreboard status UI asleep between meaningful label updates.
- Moved survival sky and astral veil epoch timing onto a shared render-clock helper, eliminating direct wall-clock reads from the sky frame bodies while keeping meditation fade timing aligned to the original epoch timestamps.
- Moved healing crystal toxic-clear/health tick expiry checks onto the shared render-clock epoch helper, so active healing projectiles no longer read the wall clock from their frame loop.
- Moved mana pickup pulse animation onto the Three render clock and replaced event-time random pulse IDs with a local counter, removing the remaining `performance.now()` reads from `Runes.tsx`.
- Moved avatar billboard frame advancement onto the Three render clock through a rendering-owned avatar animation helper, removing one interval per animated world/NPC/remote avatar while preserving sparse blink timers.
- Gated the authored mountain waterfall frame subscription behind its visible detail phase and avoided redundant visibility writes, so hidden mountain detail phases no longer keep a waterfall camera check registered.
- Moved Darrel spirit dragon sprite animation onto the Three render clock, leaving only the event-based interaction debounce on `performance.now()`.
- Changed Magic Glass Orb target-signal sampling and animated spell-menu thumbnail refreshes from fixed intervals to cancellable timeout chains while preserving the prior 160 ms and 180 ms cadences.
- Changed the rendering-owned ResizeObserver fallback and HUD layout QA metrics from fixed intervals to timeout-scheduled polling, preserving event-driven resize/orientation wakeups and change-filtered dataset publishing.
- Changed spell-dummy QA respawns from a 220 ms polling interval to exact next-respawn timeout scheduling, so the dummy test scene sleeps until a knocked-down dummy can stand up.
- Changed dormant tutorial/local grass streaming recovery from fixed mount intervals to timeout-scheduled mount steps that stop once visible cells reconcile to the current cell target.
- Confirmed the scoped game-source scan now has no `setInterval` / `clearInterval` matches under `src/game`, `src/components`, `src/App.tsx`, or `src/main.tsx`.
- Reused event and RAF timestamps in the DOM minimap/map controls path, so compact player-marker tracking and expanded-map controller repeat navigation no longer call `Date.now()` or `performance.now()` during map updates.
- Moved base-villager and persistent quest-NPC reaction ticks onto the Three.js render clock, replaced reaction cleanup with direct loops, and removed `Object.keys` / `Object.values` / `Object.entries` allocation paths from `Villagers.tsx` while leaving explicit interaction debounces intact.
- Routed water-ripple trigger, QA, cleanup, and mesh animation timing through input event timestamps, RAF timestamps, and the shared ripple render clock, removing the remaining direct wall-clock reads from `WaterRipples.tsx`.
- Reused key event and RAF timestamps across HUD map toggle, keyboard magic hold, controller repeat/remap polling, quest dialog controller navigation, launch-menu controller navigation, and voice analyzer throttling, while leaving intentional pointer-lock/remap grace clocks as explicit UI debounces.
- Replaced survival multiplayer player-count estimation and shared multiplayer sanitizer payload-presence checks with direct own-property loops instead of temporary key arrays.
- Moved the remaining player-controller sleep/slow/astral status checks inside the main frame loop onto the shared render-clock epoch helper, leaving event/projectile/session IDs on epoch timestamps.
- Removed the duplicate quest-dialog export from the `QuestPanels` editor chunk so normal dialog UI stays owned by `QuestDialogPanel.tsx`; the optional quest-editor bundle dropped from about 18.15 kB / 4.94 kB gzip to about 14.24 kB / 3.83 kB gzip.
- Fully removed the inert legacy quest-dialog JSX tail from `QuestPanels.tsx`, leaving the editor chunk with only quest-editor source.
- Reused RAF timestamps for controller spell hotbar repeat polling in `playerControllerCasting.ts` instead of sampling `performance.now()` inside the controller input loop.
- Reused render-clock timestamps for villager quest-interaction debounces and Darrel dragon dialog debounces, leaving only intentional epoch/event timestamps for cross-route gates and persisted editor metadata.
- Routed player event/projectile timing through a shared frame-epoch helper and reused `nowMs` for frame-created QA/flamethrower projectiles, reducing scattered `Date.now()` reads in `PlayerController.tsx` to a single fallback.
- Moved player spell projectile ID creation, grab IDs, QA practice IDs, blink teleport offsets, and flamethrower spread application into `playerSpellCasting.ts`, keeping randomized spell behavior in the spell system instead of scattered through `PlayerController.tsx`.
- Moved spell-dummy QA scene state transforms, dummy preservation, hit dedupe, projectile planning, respawn scheduling, and snapshot publishing into lazy `spellDummyQaSceneRuntime.ts`, while keeping the shared `spellDummyQa.ts` contract lean for normal projectile gameplay.
- Routed mana-flower collection cooldown creation through the render-clock epoch path and removed per-flower render-time wall-clock reads from the ready-state check.
- Changed engine placeable random yaw from time-based jitter to deterministic grid-position yaw, so editor previews and overlap validation stay stable when the planner reruns.
- Removed redundant wall-clock budget checks from survival procedural prewarm slices because the staged prewarm path already processes one chunk per scheduled background wake.
- Fed remote avatar interpolation from subscription-backed snapshot refs instead of frame-loop store reads, keeping the 32-player remote render path cheaper while preserving smoothing.
- Moved base-village bush blocker coordinates/corner checks out of the per-spawn-candidate path and reused the instanced bush bounding sphere during mesh setup.
- Throttled BOTW grass build/prewarm slice budget checks so mountain/hill grass generation no longer polls `performance.now()` on every blade/flower candidate while preserving the same candidate limits, preview timing, and visual output.
- Reused the latest mana render-clock epoch for mana-flower cooldown scheduling, pruning, and QA dataset publishing so `Runes.tsx` keeps one fallback wall-clock path instead of sampling `Date.now()` through every cooldown effect.
- Changed the gameplay HUD buff countdown hook to stay at `clock=0` when all buff/status expiries are zero, while still resolving nonzero expired timestamps against a real epoch before rendering status badges.
- Extracted player screen-shake runtime so camera shake no longer owns event parsing, clamp logic, expiry reset, and camera-vector math inside the main `PlayerController.tsx` body.
- Extracted player toxic status-damage buffering/sync, camera anti-clip body correction, stable collider filtering, and floor-recovery application out of the main player frame loop while preserving the same body/camera/event side effects.
- Gated navigation recorder sample construction before building per-frame array payloads in normal movement and Lily Coil traversal, so ordinary gameplay skips that telemetry work while recording is off.
- Moved HUD lobby-message cleanup scheduling into `useHudLobbyMessageCleanup.ts`, leaving `HUD.tsx` to wire state while the hook owns next-expiry removal timing.
- Routed navigation recorder samples through the player frame epoch when the recorder is active, so active recordings reuse the same timestamp source as the rest of the controller frame.
- Routed status-bolt local status expiry through the spell render-clock epoch and `spellStatusRuntime.ts`, removing direct wall-clock expiry math from the projectile renderer.
- Moved active-portal teleport cooldown state into `spellPortalRuntime.ts`, replacing the old `window.__lastTeleport` browser global with an isolated spell-system reserve helper.
- Moved the mana-flower QA hide route flag into `manaRechargeRuntime.ts`, keeping the survival/base mana renderer focused on source reconciliation and chunk keys while the spell runtime owns the browser route cache.
- Moved generated treehouse bark/plank texture caches into `treeHouseVillageTextures.ts`, replacing `window.__barkTexture` / `window.__plankTexture` globals with module-owned village texture state.
- Added a typed `getPublishedLocalPlayerPosition` bridge and routed rune/mana recharge logic through it, removing raw `window.localPlayerPos` casts from `Runes.tsx`.
- Verified the player spell projectile helper split with a focused `tsx` probe, `tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build`, PC gameplay HUD QA (`wofHudQaFailCount=0`, 3 canvases, 0 touch controls), and the `qaSpellDummies=1` route rendering all four dummy labels/health bars without runtime failure text.
- Verified the spell-dummy QA runtime split with a focused `tsx` probe, `tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build`, and an in-browser F9 QA cast: dummy A dropped from `120/120` to `96/120`, hit telemetry reached `1`, all four dummy labels remained visible, and no runtime failure text appeared. The corrected lazy split kept the production `GameWorld` chunk near `2,371.89 kB / 877.19 kB gzip` instead of pulling scene-only QA helpers into core gameplay.

Next:
- Continue reducing per-frame work, unnecessary React renders, duplicated materials/geometries, and broad system coupling.
- Split high-cost systems into isolated modules so survival terrain, vegetation, mountain villages, physics, spawning, spells, HUD panels, and editor tools cannot accidentally interfere with each other.
- Track build chunk size and keep code-splitting limited to non-core tools or menus when it helps PC/mobile startup without affecting exploration.

## 7. Multiplayer And Cross-Platform Readiness

Status: In progress

Done:
- Started the physical input-system boundary by moving shared controller/gamepad primitives under `src/game/systems/input/`, which is required before PC, Android, iOS, and later console controls can share consistent button semantics.
- Moved controller settings labels and remap rows under the input system so PC/mobile/controller menu semantics share one metadata owner.
- Moved keyboard keybind guide metadata under the input system alongside controller menu metadata.
- Moved editable-target shortcut guarding under the input system so text entry consistently blocks gameplay shortcuts, inventory controls, quest-editor hotkeys, and voice push-to-talk.
- Moved pointer-lock and fullscreen display-mode helper logic under the input system so PC browser, mobile fullscreen, and fallback mouse-look behavior share one platform boundary.
- Moved device/mobile/touch QA classification under the input system so cross-platform layout and control rules have one owner.
- Split forced mobile-layout QA from forced touchscreen-control QA, keeping PC aspect-matrix routes free of accidental mobile gameplay buttons while preserving real Android/iOS touch detection and explicit touch-control test flags.
- Moved the shared Socket.IO client, multiplayer room synchronizer, and voice chat signaling component under `src/game/network/`, giving future 32-player replication and platform voice/input rules one physical networking boundary.
- Added a gameplay network client facade for local player identity, connected-player checks, gameplay event emission, and local damage/status sync so transport details stay inside the networking system.
- Added a dedicated invite-room helper for room-code sanitizing, URL extraction, current-room reads, and LAN response typing.
- Extended the invite-room helper to own mobile invite URL generation and URL history updates, then pointed the launch screen at the same helper as HUD.
- Added a Canvas runtime QA probe so PC/mobile tests can distinguish "App/HUD mounted" from "Three.js Canvas child tree is actually ticking."
- Added a QA stale-origin guard so survival QA URLs do not inherit an old `{x:0,z:0}` browser player position before the requested chunk/local spawn applies.
- Moved survival QA observer bootstrap ownership into the QA tools boundary while preserving the `wofQaSurvivalObserver` datasets.
- Added named aspect-ratio QA profiles for PC, Android, iOS, tablet, and ultrawide checks, plus the `qaTouchLayout=1` mobile-layout override that does not imply touch controls on PC.
- Added QA-only HUD layout measuring so aspect-ratio routes can catch clipped HUD panels, missing minimap/menu/hands/touch layers, and core overlap regressions without adding polling cost to ordinary gameplay.
- Moved mobile layout runtime defaults/classes/viewport variables under the input system so PC, Android, and iOS layout rules share one platform owner.
- Moved shared player input state under the input system and survival spawn routing under the survival-world system, so PC, mobile, controller, and QA routes consume the same ownership boundaries instead of duplicating route/control behavior in the player controller.
- Moved scoreboard row/status shaping into the HUD UI boundary with network IDs supplied through the networking facade, giving future multiplayer roster/score rules one reusable row builder.
- Changed scoreboard row/status shaping to keep remote rows ordered during collection, avoiding a separate roster sort pass while preserving deterministic 32-player scoreboard order.
- Shared allocation-light player ID keys through the networking facade and removed avoidable array churn from room-state setup, status clearing, voice peer discovery, and grab-control sync paths before 32-player multiplayer work.
- Added a slice-free remote-player presence scan in the networking facade so voice proximity volume scheduling can check for non-local peers without materializing each player ID string.
- Moved survival walk QA tuning and route/snapshot helpers into the QA tools boundary, keeping automated PC/mobile route checks separate from the controller path that real players use.
- Moved player movement/camera/collider tuning into a player-controller boundary, giving PC/mobile/controller movement and future prediction/reconciliation one shared tuning surface.
- Moved controller cast/hotbar polling into the player-controller boundary while keeping fullscreen behind the touch fullscreen helper, so controller face buttons no longer share a path with browser fullscreen requests.
- Added sub-frame primary-gamepad scan sharing in the input system, keeping PC/controller polling cheaper across HUD, player movement, voice push-to-talk, launch menus, map controls, and quest dialogs without changing button mappings.
- Guarded known Rapier cleanup errors during route/settings teardown so cross-profile PC-to-mobile QA switches recover to a live canvas instead of surfacing an app crash.
- Hid magic hand layers while the full-screen engine menu is open so dev overlays do not keep extra hand canvases alive or leak behind the tool UI.
- Moved the forced HUD-state QA runtime out of the HUD renderer so spell/settings/engine/map/scoreboard layout checks can grow without adding more testing branches to the main HUD component.
- Added a generated HUD/aspect QA route matrix so cross-profile layout checks can be automated from shared data instead of hand-built browser URLs.
- Added `qaHudState=scoreboard` to the HUD/aspect route matrix and gave the roster panel direct layout telemetry, including compact touch-layout labels for phone portrait views.
- Added `qaHudState=inventory` to the HUD/aspect route matrix, with inventory modal fit checks covering PC and Android portrait-style mobile viewports.
- Verified the PC 16:9 aspect-matrix gameplay route with cached QA summaries, no PC touch controls, and `hudQaFailCount=0` after the magic-hand fill offset fix.
- Added `src/game/network/multiplayerSessionConfig.js` as the shared PC/Android/iOS multiplayer session contract with the 32-player room cap, pose sync rates, server/client tick budget targets, platform budgets, and stable join-rejection reasons.
- Reused the shared pose-rate config in player network sync, and raised the custom/survival lobby max-player controls to the shared 32-player cap while preserving the existing default lobby sizes.
- Enforced the 32-player cap in `server.js` before moving sockets between rooms, exposed the cap through `/api/health` and debug room metadata, and changed room color/state/status scans to direct loops.
- Added `src/game/network/multiplayerEventContracts.js` as the shared event contract for player pose updates, spell casts, grab control/release, armor, damage, and status effects.
- Routed the gameplay network client, server room handlers, and `NetworkManager` receive path through shared sanitizers so client payloads cannot spoof sender IDs, inject health/armor/status fields through movement updates, send unbounded damage, or broadcast oversized/invalid vectors.
- Verified the real two-client Socket.IO path keeps spell and movement events attributed to the sender socket ID while normalizing aim/spell directions and clamping damage/survival-level payloads.
- Added shared server event budgets for joins, player pose updates, spell casts, mana pulses, armor/status updates, damage, and grab control/release so one noisy socket cannot flood a 32-player room while normal high-priority grab sync remains inside the allowed budget.
- Changed shared multiplayer payload sanitizers to use direct own-property presence checks, avoiding `Object.keys(...)` arrays on accepted movement/character payloads before the 32-player networking work grows heavier.
- Added shared placeable replication contracts for engine-editor upsert/delete/snapshot events, bounded them on the server, and routed received events through `NetworkManager` into the existing placeable event bus.
- Added in-memory room-owned placed-object snapshots, join-time snapshot delivery, room cleanup for placement state, and debug room metadata for current placeable counts.
- Added `src/game/network/multiplayerServerValidation.js` for server-owned action-origin validation, marking trusted player poses on `updateMe` and rejecting spell/grab origins that claim to come from far away once the server has a pose.
- Expanded the HUD/aspect QA route matrix to cover keybind, voice, and character settings panes plus the expanded world-map page, bringing the PC/Android/iOS/tablet generated matrix to 98 routes across 7 profiles and 14 HUD states.
- Hardened multiplayer voice signaling with per-socket server event budgets, bounded SDP/ICE payload sanitizers, room-target validation, offer/answer type checks, and server-owned `fromId` relay identity so clients cannot spoof voice sender IDs.
- Added server-side combat-damage target validation: raw toxic `damageHealth` can only target the emitting socket, while `hitPlayer` combat damage is limited to self-targeted reports or plausible spell-range attacker/target positions.
- Added server-side status-effect target validation: victim-reported self status still works, direct remote status requires a plausible range, and status clearing is self-only so clients cannot clear another player's poison/acid/slow/sleep effects.
- Gated the lazy `VoiceChat` runtime mount behind `voiceChatEnabled` or explicit voice QA route flags so ordinary solo survival no longer requests the voice chunk while voice is off.
- Added `voiceChatRouteFlags.ts` so voice soundboard/autostart QA flags are parsed once when the lazy voice runtime mounts instead of being recomputed during voice renders.
- Gated the voice proximity-volume refresh loop behind actual non-local player IDs so voice-enabled empty rooms do not keep a 120 ms audio-volume interval awake.
- Changed remote avatars to use one Zustand player snapshot subscription plus frame-local refs for interpolation, removing per-remote per-frame store lookups and snapping each remote to its first valid transform before smoothing begins.
- Moved remote-avatar status expiry clocks into `remotePlayerRuntime.ts`, with idle remote players staying at `clock=0` until they actually have a timed slow/sleep/poison/acid status.

Next:
- Continue toward authoritative networking, replication boundaries, input abstraction, prediction/reconciliation, and deeper server-side gameplay validation around the new 32-player session cap.
- PC, Android, and iOS testing come first; console readiness comes after the core architecture is stable.
